ajmarshall · Community · 2021-04-15

Yes to all. Nothing stops a planet being in a supernova, and an exploration card can give you the Mirage there. A supernova can be activated; it only stops ships moving into or through it, so placing structures, producing, committing ground forces with the Sardakk commander, or entering with Nav Suite all work, and ships can always move out.

### muaat-hero-nova-seed

It destroys only other players' units; Muaat's own stay, the war sun included, and can leave later, since nothing stops ships moving out of a supernova. It works only on the system the war sun moved into, never one it passed through.

### direct-hit-vs-mechs

### exploration-cards-public
Are exploration cards public, and what happens when a deck runs out?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

hooliganj · Community · 2020-12-05

AgentDrake · Community · 2020-12-05

Vidyaraja · Community · 2020-11-29

IncrediSteve · Community · 2021-04-15

They resolve openly; relic fragments are kept face up and attachments go on planets, while other cards are discarded. When a deck runs out, shuffle its discards into a new deck; used relic fragments are purged, so they never come back.

### ion-storm-flipping
When does the Ion Storm flip, and does using a matching wormhole in the same system flip it?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree and relay an emailed ruling from FFG; no official post on BGG.

Clipper · Community · 2021-04-18

DistantOasis · Community · 2021-04-18

Neurosion · Community · 2021-06-16

hooliganj · Community · 2021-04-05

pkitty · Community · 2021-04-06disagrees

MollyMae · Community · 2022-01-11

It flips whenever any player's ships move into or out of a system through it, so you can exit through it and later use the other side, even in one long move. With several wormhole routes, the moving player chooses which, if any, they use, which decides whether the Ion Storm flips, per a compiled developer answer.

### scanlink-needs-unit-on-planet

ajmarshall · Community · 2021-04-19

AgentDrake · Community · 2021-04-19

Marcial · Community · 2021-09-23

Each time you activate a system, you may explore one planet there that already contains any of your units (infantry, mech, PDS or dock), even one explored before, so the same planets can be explored again and again. It won't explore planets your ships merely fly past.

### heroes-movement-nexus-invasion
How do the Argent and L1Z1X heroes move ships, and can they reach the inactive Wormhole Nexus or invade?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

Old_Man_gi · Community · 2021-01-08

IncrediSteve · Community · 2021-01-11

pkitty · Community · 2021-03-13

IncrediSteve · Community · 2022-04-28

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-09

IncrediSteve · Community · 2025-08-05

Only by the hero's text: no path or move value, from any number of systems for the Argent hero, ignoring command tokens in the systems they leave. So the Argent hero can move ships into the Wormhole Nexus, which flips to its active side at the end of that action. The ships can carry units starting with them, but can't pick up along a path, and since it isn't a tactical action there's no invasion and nothing like Dark Energy Tap triggers.

### mahact-commander-how-it-works
How does the Mahact commander work?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

AgentDrake · Community · 2020-12-05

Jake4Prezident · Community · 2020-12-05

IncrediSteve · Community · 2021-07-30

You may activate a system that already holds your command token; you then remove both your tokens from it and your turn ends at once, so nothing else, production included, happens there that turn. In effect you spend a turn and a token to undo an earlier activation, so the system can be activated again later.

### nekro-dimensional-tears
Do Nekro-copied Dimensional Tears affect the Vuil'raith, and do they count for the Vuil'raith hero?

Best reading — unconfirmed

One experienced player's reading; no official ruling or wider agreement.

hooliganj · Community · 2020-12-15

Tears only hinder other players' units: Vuil'raith ignore their own, Nekro ignore Nekro's, but Vuil'raith are affected by Nekro tears. All tears on the board count for the Vuil'raith hero.

### vuilraith-captured-units
What are captured units good for, and how do Amalgamation and Riftmeld use them?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

ajmarshall · Community · 2022-06-01

ajmarshall · Community · 2020-11-23

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-10

hooliganj · Community · 2020-12-15

IncrediSteve · Community · 2022-05-06

zamoose · Community · 2025-03-05

Returning a captured unit of a type lets the Vuil'raith produce one unit of that type without paying (Amalgamation) or ignore prerequisites on its upgrade (Riftmeld). Each captured unit or token gives one unit, even fighters or infantry, and its printed cost still counts toward limits like Integrated Economy's. Any captured unit of the base type works, a Prototype War Sun counting as a war sun. Amalgamation is producing, so it can trigger Sling Relay.

### vuilraith-flagship-scope
What does the Vuil'raith flagship capture?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

Heiko Doubleu · Community · 2023-02-06

IncrediSteve · Community · 2021-05-20

Clipper · Community · 2021-04-18

IncrediSteve · Community · 2021-04-18

justbj · Community · 2021-04-18disagrees

Every other non-structure unit destroyed in its system, the Cabal's own included, even outside combat: Bombardment, Space Cannon, action cards, agendas. It never captures itself. If the Yin flagship blows up the system, the Cabal flagship is destroyed too but still captures everything else destroyed there, the Yin flagship included.

### titans-sleeper-details
Which side of the sleeper token is used, when does Terragenesis move one, and can Awaken work with no PDS left?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

IncrediSteve · Community · 2022-01-21

AgentDrake · Community · 2022-05-07

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-08

StrangeBeacon · Community · 2025-10-20

IncrediSteve · Community · 2022-07-02

Old_Man_gi · Community · 2022-05-08

Use the plain sleeper side; the Titans-logo side marks ownership, and the hero and note attachment tokens aren't sleepers. A planet holds one sleeper; Awaken says 'one or more' because a system may have several planets. Replaced sleepers return and can be placed again, and Terragenesis moves one only once all yours are on the board. Awaken takes a PDS from reinforcements and isn't production, so with no PDS left you can't awaken.

### reaching-wormhole-nexus

Rout needs a legal destination; without one it does nothing and the ships keep fighting, they aren't destroyed. If the combat ends before the retreat, for example because Assault Cannon kills the last ship at the start, no retreat happens.

### seven-eight-player-extra-tiles

### exploration-basics

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

IncrediSteve · Community · 2021-12-12

TinTeardrop · Community · 2021-12-13

Clipper · Community · 2021-12-05

Gylthinel · Community · 2021-12-17

When you gain control of a planet nobody controlled, draw a card from the exploration deck matching its trait; this happens before 'after you gain control' effects like Integrated Economy or Pre-Fab Arcologies, which you then order as you like. Frontier tokens mark planetless systems still to be explored. Abilities like Naaz-Rokha's let you re-explore planets you hold.

### edge-of-board-with-hyperlanes

### anomaly-home-system-setup-adjacency

### xxcha-hero-how-it-works
How does the Xxcha hero resolve agendas, and can Xxcha play riders on them?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

Cacotopos · Community · 2021-09-11

Clipper · Community · 2021-09-12

Clipper · Community · 2021-09-12

McBartsomeville · Community · 2021-09-13

Clipper · Community · 2021-09-13

Clipper · Community · 2021-09-13

Resolve the hero's text in order: any law discard comes first, before you look at the agendas. Then you pick two of the drawn agendas and choose one outcome for each as if you had cast a single vote; nobody votes and you can't abstain, and the rest are discarded. No other player may use abilities during it, but the agendas are revealed, so Xxcha can play riders such as Imperial Rider on them. One player argues nothing is revealed.

### xxcha-hero-follow-up-effects
After the Xxcha hero resolves agendas in the action phase, do exhausted planets stay exhausted, and does Public Execution carry over?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

Clipper · Community · 2021-09-13

McBartsomeville · Community · 2021-09-13

ajmarshall · Community · 2021-04-15

Kazadoom · Community · 2021-04-15

Planets exhausted for any votes it triggers stay exhausted until readied normally. A player elected for Public Execution can still vote in the next agenda phase. Transactions during the hero's action still need neighbors, since it isn't the agenda phase. Agenda-phase secret objectives such as Drive the Debate can't be scored from it, since it isn't the agenda phase.

### xxcha-commander-vs-cannot-vote
What does the Xxcha commander allow, and does it beat Political Secret or a rider?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

AgentDrake · Community · 2021-01-23

pedro2112 · Community · 2021-01-23

ajmarshall · Community · 2021-04-15

Kazadoom · Community · 2021-04-15

sigmazero13 · Community · 2021-03-22

Kazadoom · Community · 2021-03-22

Each planet you exhaust to vote gives 1 extra vote, so even a 0-influence planet gives 1. It is a lasting ability that permits voting, and such abilities beat one-time effects, even ones that say 'cannot', so Xxcha can vote despite a rider or Political Secret. Through the Xxcha alliance even the Nekro can vote.

### deadly-plot-timing
When is Deadly Plot played?

Best reading — unconfirmed

One experienced player's reading; no official ruling or wider agreement.

Clipper · Community · 2021-08-25

Clipper · Community · 2021-08-25

After all votes are cast and any tie is broken, and after the Legal Text cards, but before the outcome resolves, so before any dice for it are rolled. The agenda is discarded with no effect, and predictions can't pay out since that isn't an outcome.

### sardakk-commander-no-ships

### mentak-hero-mid-combat
How does the Mentak hero work, and can it be escaped?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

Clipper · Community · 2021-03-22

sigmazero13 · Community · 2025-05-11

IncrediSteve · Community · 2025-05-11

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-17

zamoose · Community · 2020-12-17

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-03

Purge it at the start of space combat; for the whole combat, each enemy ship destroyed gives you a matching ship, before a winner is decided, so the combat can continue even if your other ships died. The opponent can limit it by retreating, or escape before any losses with Skilled Retreat, which comes at the start of a combat round, after the hero.

### moving-through-is-not-moving-into
Do abilities that trigger on ships moving into a system, or through a wormhole, count systems merely passed through?

Clipper · Community · 2021-10-17

No — only the system where movement ends is 'moved into', so the Empyrean commander returns at most one token per move. Likewise, a ship only moves through a wormhole for the Creuss commander if it actually uses the wormhole's connection; crossing a wormhole system doesn't count.

### mahact-hero-benediction
How does the Mahact hero's Benediction work?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

Finrodimus · Community · 2020-11-12

AgentDrake · Community · 2020-11-12

AgentDrake · Community · 2020-11-14

Geoff Watson · Community · 2020-11-15

ajmarshall · Community · 2021-04-15

Zejety · Community · 2021-01-21

Pick one system, with your fleet or another player's, and move all units in its space area (fighters, ground forces in space and Saar docks included) into one adjacent system containing another player's ships, where they must fight. The moved ships' owner must be able to enter that system, so no pushing them into asteroid fields or supernovas they can't enter, and Creuss's alpha-beta adjacency can't be used. Neither side may retreat or use abilities that move ships, Skilled Retreat included.

### nomad-memoria-mech-paths
How does the Nomad flagship Memoria's adjacency to mechs work?

Best reading — unconfirmed

Players disagree; this is the better-supported reading, with no official ruling.

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-21

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-21

IncrediSteve · Community · 2020-12-21

IncrediSteve · Community · 2025-12-31

IncrediSteve · Community · 2022-05-20

bigGameGeek · Community · 2020-12-21disagrees

The Memoria alone treats every system containing a Nomad mech as adjacent, mech on a planet or in space, so it can reach that system for 1 move however far away, though enemy ships there still block it. A system next to the mech costs 2. It is not a wormhole: other players' Space Cannon can't use it and it doesn't work in reverse. A mech being carried in the same move doesn't count, since ships move together, but the Memoria can pick up mechs from systems it enters.

### aerie-hololattice-production
How does Aerie Hololattice's Production 1 work?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

hooliganj · Community · 2026-01-10

toskk · Community · 2026-01-10

IncrediSteve · Community · 2022-09-16

Clipper · Community · 2023-02-09

isterbat · Community · 2024-06-27

Marcial · Community · 2024-06-27

Each planet with your structure gets Production 1 as if it were a unit, which raises the system's total and lets a planet with only a PDS produce. It isn't actually a unit, so effects that look for a unit with Production, such as the Arborec commander, don't see it, and it adds nothing to the Warfare secondary, which uses only one space dock's Production.

### fracture-seven-systems
Is The Fracture 3 systems or 7, and is it adjacent to the galaxy it touches?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree and relay an emailed ruling from FFG; no official post on BGG.

GhostTrail · Community · 2025-10-28

IncrediSteve · Community · 2025-10-29

sigmazero13 · Community · 2025-11-06

IncrediSteve · Community · 2026-01-10

iswearihaveajob · Community · 2025-10-28

Bluedevil310 · Community · 2025-10-29disagrees

Most players treat each hex as its own system, making 7, and players report the developer said the same. Fracture systems that touch are adjacent to each other but not to the galaxy tiles beside them; you reach it only through ingress and egress tokens. It can sit anywhere, and the galaxy tiles it touches stay edge systems, as the developer confirmed by email and reply, while every Fracture system also counts as on the edge.

### te-warfare-card

zamoose · Community · 2026-02-15

The primary lets you redistribute your tokens and perform a tactical action without placing a command token, even in a system that already holds yours or a Diplomacy token, so newly produced ships can move later that round; that system still counts as activated. You can move ships into an already-activated system that way, but not out of one. The secondary produces with all your units in your home system.

### nekro-breakthrough-flagships

axibis · Community · 2026-04-23

IncrediSteve · Community · 2026-04-24

There's no limit: whenever you'd gain another player's technology through a faction ability, you may put an Assimilator Z token on their faction sheet instead, and your flagship gains that flagship's text abilities. It isn't a technology, so the Nekro commander doesn't trigger. Unit abilities like Deploy, Space Cannon or Sustain Damage aren't text abilities, so copying flagships that rely on them gains nothing.

### thunders-edge-expedition

Whoever holds the most slices then puts that many of their infantry on it; on a tie, the player who claimed the last slice chooses among the tied players.

### coexistence-rules
How does coexistence work?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

hooliganj · Community · 2026-01-07

ArcTheKnight · Community · 2025-10-31

IncrediSteve · Community · 2026-02-21

AgentDrake · Community · 2025-12-07

StrangeBeacon · Community · 2026-03-13

StrangeBeacon · Community · 2026-03-13

Only units with an effect granting it can coexist, mostly from the Deepwrought; Deepwrought may choose it whenever any player commits ground forces to the planet. The player whose units triggered it never controls the planet or takes its card, though they count as controlling it for objectives and neighbors; if they held it, they lose control. Coexisting structures still work but count as blockaded, and you can't build on a planet you only coexist on.

Planetary Shield on the planet protects everyone there from Bombardment, both players can fire Space Cannon Defense at an invader, and Support for the Throne still counts coexisting units.

### crimson-breaches
How do Crimson Rebellion breaches work?

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

hooliganj · Community · 2026-08-21

SeanSealey · Community · 2026-10-06

IncrediSteve · Community · 2026-02-21

A breach goes in the combat's system at the end of combat, whoever won, if one of your destroyers is in range then; it needn't have fought. At the end of the status phase a player with ships there may remove any or all active breaches, never inactive ones. Their mech can deploy through an active breach in the commit step, from reinforcements, even with no other units there, and needs no ground combat.

AgentDrake · Community · 2025-11-23

Yes — the scar stops unit abilities being used but doesn't remove them, and the commander is a text ability that only needs a unit with Production.

### breakthroughs-not-technologies
Are breakthroughs technologies, and do their passive abilities work while exhausted?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

StrangeBeacon · Community · 2026-06-03

sigmazero13 · Community · 2026-01-07

GhostTrail · Community · 2026-01-07

StrangeBeacon · Community · 2026-01-06

They aren't technologies, so a breakthrough like Mentak's Corsair doesn't count as a unit upgrade or for anything that cares about technologies. Their passive abilities keep working while exhausted. Synergy lets a technology of one linked colour count as the other for colour objectives.

### alliance-another-player
In the Alliance variant, do 'another player' abilities trigger on my ally?

Best reading — unconfirmed

One experienced player's reading; no official ruling or wider agreement.

legosrock · Community · 2025-11-02

legosrock · Community · 2025-11-03

No — an ally counts as 'any player' but not 'another player', so effects like the Empyrean alliance or Naalu Foresight don't trigger on them, while 'any player' effects still do.

### te-small-clarifications
Are there quick answers for Watchtower, Reflect, Garbozia's legendary ability, Brilliance, Ignis and Ral Nel Linkships?

Community consensus

Several experienced players agree; there is no official ruling on BGG.

IncrediSteve · Community · 2026-08-26

hooliganj · Community · 2026-02-22

GhostTrail · Community · 2026-05-01

StrangeBeacon · Community · 2026-03-16

StrangeBeacon · Community · 2025-11-29

GhostTrail · Community · 2025-12-22

The Watchtower is one system, so one anomaly. Reflect lets you choose per trade good, in sequence. Garbozia's legendary ability lets you choose which discarded action card to take. Brilliance's chosen player gains their own breakthrough. Ignis places a token without activating, so Nullification Field can't answer it. Each Linkship II rolls one Space Cannon die however many PDS share its space.

### twilights-fall-home-system-picks
In Twilight's Fall premade-map setup, who picks a home system first?

Best reading — unconfirmed

One experienced player's reading; no official ruling or wider agreement.

IncrediSteve · Community · 2025-11-03

The speaker picks last; the others pick from the lowest initiative number among non-speakers.
